Mathlib.FieldTheory.AbelRuffini

solvableByRaddefMathlib.FieldTheory.AbelRuffini, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $F$ and $E$ be sets. Then $\mathrm{solvableByRad}$ applied to $F$ and $E$ is an intermediate field of $F$ and $E$ defined as the infimum of $\operatorname{ofPred}$ applied to the function that maps $s$ to the proposition that for all elements $x_{3}$ of $E$, for all natural numbers $n$, if $n \neq 0$, then if $x_{3} ^ {n}$ is an element of $s$, then $x_{3}$ is an element of $s$.
Formula
$F , E \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{solvableByRad} (F , E):= \operatorname{sInf} (\operatorname{ofPred} (\lambda s . \forall x_{3} \in E . \forall n \in \Natural . n \neq 0 \rightarrow \MembershipmemMACRoAA {s}{x_{3} ^ {n}}\rightarrow \MembershipmemMACRoAA {s}{x_{3}}))\in \mathrm{IntermediateField} (F , E)$
French
Soient $F$ et $E$ des ensembles. Alors $\mathrm{solvableByRad}$ appliqué à $F$ et $E$ est un corps intermédiaire de $F$ et $E$ défini comme l'infimum de $\operatorname{ofPred}$ appliqué à la fonction qui envoie $s$ à la proposition que pour tous les éléments $x_{3}$ de $E$, pour tous les nombres naturels $n$, si $n \neq 0$, alors si $x_{3} ^ {n}$ est un élément de $s$, alors $x_{3}$ est un élément de $s$.

Mathlib.FieldTheory.AbsoluteGaloisGroup

Field.absoluteGaloisGroupdefMathlib.FieldTheory.AbsoluteGaloisGroup, type
Wording
absolute Galois group of K (from the name)
English
Let $K$ be a set. Then an absolute Galois group of $K$ is an element of the function that maps $K$ to the set of algebra isomorphisms of the set of algebraic closures of $K$ and the set of algebraic closures of $K$.
Formula
$K \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{absoluteGaloisGroup} (K):= \lambda K . \mathrm{AlgEquiv} (K , \mathrm{AlgebraicClosure} (K), \mathrm{AlgebraicClosure} (K))$
French
Soit $K$ un ensemble. Alors un groupe de Galois absolu de $K$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $K$ à l'ensemble d'isomorphismes d'algèbres de l'ensemble de clôtures algébriques de $K$ et l'ensemble de clôtures algébriques de $K$.
Field.absoluteGaloisGroup.mapdefMathlib.FieldTheory.AbsoluteGaloisGroup, function or constant
Wording
the map of f (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a ring homomorphism of $K$ and $L$. Then the map of $f$ is a continuous monoid homomorphism of the set of absolute Galois groups of $L$ and the set of absolute Galois groups of $K$ defined as the map of $K$ and $L$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{RingHom} (K , L)\vdash \operatorname{map} (f):= \operatorname{mapOfAlgebra} (K , L)\in \mathrm{ContinuousMonoidHom} (\operatorname{absoluteGaloisGroup} (L), \operatorname{absoluteGaloisGroup} (K))$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme d'anneaux de $K$ et $L$. Alors l'application de $f$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes continu de l'ensemble de groupes de Galois absolus de $L$ et l'ensemble de groupes de Galois absolus de $K$ défini comme l'application de $K$ et $L$.
Field.absoluteGaloisGroup.mapOfAlgebradefMathlib.FieldTheory.AbsoluteGaloisGroup, function or constant
Wording
the map of K and L (from the name)
English
Let $K$ and $L$ be sets. Then the map of $K$ and $L$ is a continuous monoid homomorphism of the set of absolute Galois groups of $L$ and the set of absolute Galois groups of $K$.
Formula
$K , L \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{mapOfAlgebra} (K , L)\in \mathrm{ContinuousMonoidHom} (\operatorname{absoluteGaloisGroup} (L), \operatorname{absoluteGaloisGroup} (K))$
French
Soient $K$ et $L$ des ensembles. Alors l'application de $K$ et $L$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes continu de l'ensemble de groupes de Galois absolus de $L$ et l'ensemble de groupes de Galois absolus de $K$.
Field.absoluteGaloisGroupAbelianizationdefMathlib.FieldTheory.AbsoluteGaloisGroup, type
Wording
absolute Galois group abelianization of K (from the name)
English
Let $K$ be a set. Then an absolute Galois group abelianization of $K$ is an element of the function that maps $K$ to the set of topological abelianizations of the set of absolute Galois groups of $K$.
Formula
$K \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{absoluteGaloisGroupAbelianization} (K):= \lambda K . \mathrm{TopologicalAbelianization} (\operatorname{absoluteGaloisGroup} (K))$
French
Soit $K$ un ensemble. Alors une abélianisation de groupes de Galois absolue de $K$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $K$ à l'ensemble d'abélianisations topologiques de l'ensemble de groupes de Galois absolus de $K$.

Wording
the algebraic closure of i (from the name)
English
Let $i$ be an algebra isomorphism of $E$ and $K$. Then the algebraic closure of $i$ is an algebra isomorphism of $\SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\mathrm{algebraicClosure} (F , E)}{x_{5}})}$ and $\SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\mathrm{algebraicClosure} (F , K)}{x_{6}})}$ defined as the algebra isomorphism of $i$.
Formula
$i \in \mathrm{AlgEquiv} (F , E , K)\vdash \operatorname{algebraicClosure} (i):= \operatorname{algEquivOfAlgEquiv} (i)\in \mathrm{AlgEquiv} (F , \SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {\mathrm{algebraicClosure} (F , E)}{x_{5}})}, \SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {\mathrm{algebraicClosure} (F , K)}{x_{6}})})$
French

Alors la clôture algébrique de $i$ est un isomorphisme d'algèbres de $\SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\mathrm{algebraicClosure} (F , E)}{x_{5}})}$ et $\SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\mathrm{algebraicClosure} (F , K)}{x_{6}})}$ défini comme l'isomorphisme d'algèbres de $i$.
algebraicClosuredefMathlib.FieldTheory.AlgebraicClosure, function or constant
Wording
the algebraic closure of F and E (from the name)
English
Let $F$ and $E$ be sets. Then the algebraic closure of $F$ and $E$ is an intermediate field of $F$ and $E$ defined as the intermediate field of the integral closure of $F$ and $E$.
Formula
$F , E \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{algebraicClosure} (F , E):= \operatorname{toIntermediateField} (\mathrm{integralClosure} (F , E))\in \mathrm{IntermediateField} (F , E)$
French
Soient $F$ et $E$ des ensembles. Alors la clôture algébrique de $F$ et $E$ est un corps intermédiaire de $F$ et $E$ défini comme le corps intermédiaire de la clôture entière de $F$ et $E$.

Wording
the algebra isomorphism of i (from the name)

FirstOrder.genericPolyMapSurjOnOfInjOndefMathlib.FieldTheory.AxGrothendieck, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $x_{2}$ be a formula of the ring and $\mathrm{Sum}$ applied to $x_{1}$ and $x$. Let $\mathrm{mons}$ be a function from elements of $x$ to finite sets of $\FinsuppMACRoQ {x}{\Natural}$. Then $\operatorname{genericPolyMapSurjOnOfInjOn}$ applied to $x_{2}$ and $\mathrm{mons}$ is a sentence of the ring.
Formula
$x_{2} \in \operatorname{Formula} (\operatorname{ring} , \mathrm{Sum} (x_{1} , x)), \mathrm{mons} \in x \rightarrow \mathrm{Finset} (\FinsuppMACRoX {x}{\Natural})\vdash \operatorname{genericPolyMapSurjOnOfInjOn} (x_{2} , \mathrm{mons})\in \operatorname{Sentence} (\operatorname{ring})$
French
Soit $x_{2}$ une formule de l'anneau et $\mathrm{Sum}$ appliqué à $x_{1}$ et $x$. Soit $\mathrm{mons}$ une fonction des éléments de $x$ à des ensembles finis de $\FinsuppMACRoQ {x}{\Natural}$. Alors $\operatorname{genericPolyMapSurjOnOfInjOn}$ appliqué à $x_{2}$ et $\mathrm{mons}$ est une phrase de l'anneau.

The embedding equivalence product is an equivalence from the set of embeddings of $F$ and $E$ to the set of functions from types $i$ of the order of the rank of $F$ and $E$ to factors of $\operatorname{some}$ applied to $i$.

$\operatorname{embEquivPi} \in \EquivMACRoAL {\operatorname{Emb} (F , E)}{(\mathrm{Elem} (\operatorname{ToType} (\operatorname{ord} (\operatorname{rank} (F , E))))\rightarrow \operatorname{factor} (\operatorname{some} (i)))}$

Field.Emb.Cardinal.embFunctordefMathlib.FieldTheory.CardinalEmb, function or constant
Wording
the embedding functor of f (from the name)
English
Let $F$ and $E$ be sets. Assume that $i \leq j$. Formula

French

Let $K$ and $L$ be sets. Then the Galois basis of $K$ and $L$ is a filter basis of the set of algebra isomorphisms of $L$ and $L$.
Formula
$K , L \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{galBasis} (K , L)\in \mathrm{FilterBasis} (\mathrm{AlgEquiv} (K , L , L))$
French
Soient $K$ et $L$ des ensembles. Alors la base de Galois de $K$ et $L$ est une base de filtres de l'ensemble d'isomorphismes d'algèbres de $L$ et $L$.

Wording
the separable degree of f (from the docstring)
English
Let $f$ be a polynomial of $F$. Then the separable degree of $f$ is a natural number defined as the cardinality of the finite set of $\operatorname{aroots}$ applied to $f$ and the set of splitting fields of $f$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{Polynomial} (F)\vdash \operatorname{natSepDegree} (f):= \FinsetcardMACRoT {\operatorname{toFinset} (\operatorname{aroots} (f , \operatorname{SplittingField} (f)))}\in \Natural$
French

Mathlib.FieldTheory.SplittingField.Construction

Polynomial.IsSplittingField.algEquivdefMathlib.FieldTheory.SplittingField.Construction, function or constant
Wording
the algebra isomorphism of L and f (from the name)
English
Let $L$ be a set. Let $f$ be a polynomial of $K$. Then the algebra isomorphism of $L$ and $f$ is an algebra isomorphism of $L$ and the set of splitting fields of $f$.
Formula
$L \in \mathrm{Set} , f \in \mathrm{Polynomial} (K)\vdash \operatorname{algEquiv} (L , f)\in \mathrm{AlgEquiv} (K , L , \operatorname{SplittingField} (f))$
French
Soit $L$ un ensemble. Soit $f$ un polynôme de $K$. Alors l'isomorphisme d'algèbres de $L$ et $f$ est un isomorphisme d'algèbres de $L$ et l'ensemble de corps de scindages de $f$.
Polynomial.SplittingFielddefMathlib.FieldTheory.SplittingField.Construction, type
Wording
splitting field of f (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a polynomial of $K$. Then a splitting field of $f$ is an element of the function that maps $f$ to the set of quotients of the set of multivariate polynomials of $\operatorname{SplittingFieldAux}$ applied to the natural degree of $f$ and $f$ and $K$ and the kernel of $\operatorname{toRingHom}$ applied to the algebra evaluation of $\mathrm{id}$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{Polynomial} (K)\vdash \operatorname{SplittingField} (f):= \lambda f . \operatorname{Quotient} (\mathrm{MvPolynomial} (\operatorname{SplittingFieldAux} (\operatorname{natDegree} (f), f), K), \operatorname{ker} (\operatorname{toRingHom} (\operatorname{aeval} (\mathrm{id}))))$
French
Soit $f$ un polynôme de $K$. Alors un corps de scindages de $f$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $f$ à l'ensemble de quotients de l'ensemble de polynômes multivariés de $\operatorname{SplittingFieldAux}$ appliqué à le degré naturel de $f$ et $f$ et $K$ et le noyau de $\operatorname{toRingHom}$ appliqué à l'évaluation d'algèbres de $\mathrm{id}$.
Polynomial.SplittingField.liftdefMathlib.FieldTheory.SplittingField.Construction, function or constant
Wording
the lift of f and hb (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a polynomial of $K$ such that $\operatorname{Splits}$ holds for the map of the algebra map of $K$ and $L$ and $f$. Then the lift of $f$ and $\mathrm{hb}$ is an algebra homomorphism of the set of splitting fields of $f$ and $L$ defined as the lift of $f$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{Polynomial} (K), \operatorname{Splits} (\operatorname{map} (\operatorname{algebraMap} (K , L), f))\vdash \operatorname{lift} (f , \mathrm{hb}):= \operatorname{lift} (\operatorname{SplittingField} (f), f , \mathrm{hb})\in \mathrm{AlgHom} (K , \operatorname{SplittingField} (f), L)$
French
Soit $f$ un polynôme de $K$ tel que $\operatorname{Splits}$ tienne pour l'application de l'application d'algèbres de $K$ et $L$ et $f$. Alors le relèvement de $f$ et $\mathrm{hb}$ est un homomorphisme d'algèbres de l'ensemble de corps de scindages de $f$ et $L$ défini comme le relèvement de $f$.
Polynomial.factordefMathlib.FieldTheory.SplittingField.Construction, function or constant
Wording
the factor of f (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a polynomial of $K$. Then the factor of $f$ is a polynomial of $K$ defined as $\mathrm{dite}$ applied to the proposition that there exists a polynomial $g$ of $K$, such that $g$ is irreducible and $g$ divides $f$, the function that maps $H$ to $\operatorname{choose}$ applied to $H$ and the function that maps $H_{1}$ to $\operatorname{X}$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{Polynomial} (K)\vdash \operatorname{factor} (f):= \mathrm{dite} (\exists g \in \mathrm{Polynomial} (K). \mathrm{Irreducible} (g)\wedge (\divides {g}{f}), \lambda H . \operatorname{choose} (H), \lambda H_{1} . \operatorname{X})\in \mathrm{Polynomial} (K)$
French
Soit $f$ un polynôme de $K$. Alors le facteur de $f$ est un polynôme de $K$ défini comme $\mathrm{dite}$ appliqué à la proposition qu'il existe un polynôme $g$ de $K$ tel que $g$ soit irréductible et $g$ divise $f$, la fonction qui envoie $H$ à $\operatorname{choose}$ appliqué à $H$ et la fonction qui envoie $H_{1}$ à $\operatorname{X}$.

no wording
English

Then $\operatorname{removeFactor}$ applied to $f$ is a polynomial of $\mathrm{AdjoinRoot}$ applied to the factor of $f$ defined as $\operatorname{divByMonic}$ applied to the map of $\operatorname{of}$ applied to the factor of $f$ and $f$ and the difference between $\operatorname{X}$ and $\operatorname{C}$ applied to the adjoined root of the factor of $f$.

Mathlib.FieldTheory.SplittingField.IsSplittingField

Polynomial.IsSplittingFieldclassMathlib.FieldTheory.SplittingField.IsSplittingField, predicate
Wording
f is a splitting field (from the name)
English
Let $K$ and $L$ be sets. Let $f$ be a polynomial of $K$. Then we can say that $f$ is a splitting field.
Formula
$K , L \in \mathrm{Set} , f \in \mathrm{Polynomial} (K)\vdash \operatorname{IsSplittingField} (K , L , f)\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soient $K$ et $L$ des ensembles. Soit $f$ un polynôme de $K$. Alors nous pouvons dire que $f$ est un corps de scindages.

Wording
the lift of f (from the name)
English

Formula
$L \in \mathrm{Set} , f \in \mathrm{Polynomial} (K), \operatorname{Splits} (\operatorname{map} (\operatorname{algebraMap} (K , F), f))\vdash \operatorname{lift} (L , f , \mathrm{hf})\in \mathrm{AlgHom} (K , L , F)$
French
